# Seven boundary points: complete closure of the remaining 3/5 line positions

This note excludes every single-exterior N7 configuration with h<=e
whose OP line has a three-vertex positive arc other than FGH. The FGH
position at all heights is already excluded in N7_THREE_FIVE_TOP.md;
N7_THREE_FIVE_UPPER_HEIGHT.md excludes all positions with h>=e.
Together these three notes close the entire single-exterior N7 3/5
line class, with minimum ordinary triangle area strictly below
93/2000<A*.

The proof below has 93 fixed cubic contradictions and two direct
heptagon-edge contradictions. The literal sign paths form seven complete
binary trees. All 27,884 positive integer terms, every factor, every
sign used, and every occupancy witness are printed. The optional
standard-library checker `check_n7_three_five_lower_height.py` reads the
manuscript and reconstructs them. No search, optimization, or unprinted
enumeration is a premise.

## 1. Domain and the common geometric rules

All areas are doubled. Put s=93/1000 and K=149/20<Gamma2. Use the
single-exterior normalization from N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md:

    A=(0,a), B=(b,0), C=(c,0), D=(1,d),
    E=(1,e), F=(f,1), G=(g,z), H=(0,h),
    O=(x,y), P=(u,v), U=u-x, V=v-y,
    0<a<h<1, 0<b<c<1, 0<d<e<1,
    b<=f<=c, d<=h, a<=e, 0<g<f, h<z<1, h<=e.

G is strictly above HF. The eight actual hull vertices are in the
strict convex cyclic order A,...,H. O,P lie strictly inside the contact
heptagon ABCDEFH. Assume every actual unsigned triangle area is at
least s. Thus every actual determinant is nonzero.

Orient OP so that its three positive vertices form the named arc T;
its other five vertices are negative. For a hull vertex I put d_I=IOP.
The seven literal arc positions treated here are

    ABC, BCD, CDE, DEF, EFG, GHA, HAB.

No square symmetry is used to identify these positions. The chord
joining the two crossed edges puts O,P inside the five-vertex polygon
formed by T and its two exterior neighbours. Both points are outside
the triangle formed by T: that triangle is strictly on the positive
side of their line. The positive outer-edge tests therefore place
both points on the positive side of the chord from the first to the
last vertex of T.

All further signs follow by these exact rules:

1. Every cyclic hull triple is positive. Every edge of ABCDEFH and of
   the stated containing five-vertex polygon has a positive determinant
   with O and P. The preceding endpoint chord tests are positive too.
2. Each four-consecutive-vertex cap has capacity one by
   N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md. For its endpoint chord IJ the cap is the
   negative side. If d_I<0<d_J then IJO>0; if d_I>0>d_J then IJP>0.
   Otherwise the identity IJP-IJO=d_I-d_J would put both points in
   the cap.
3. That same exact identity propagates any known chord sign in its
   increasing or decreasing direction along OP when d_I,d_J have
   opposite signs.
4. Three positive triangle-edge tests imply strict containment.
   Every chord half-plane containing all three vertices then contains
   the interior point, by its positive barycentric coordinates.
5. Each consecutive three-vertex or four-vertex cap, and each of the
   eight triangles AEF,BEF,ABE,ABF,CDG,CGH,CDH,DGH, has capacity one.
   The latter is N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md. If one point occupies such
   a polygon and all but one edge tests for the other are positive,
   the remaining test is negative.
6. For T0=O or P with ordinate y_T0, use the exact identities

       z BHT0=h BGT0-y_T0[b(z-h)+hg],
       DFT0=(1-d)CFT0+[1-c+d(c-f)](1-y_T0).

   Their positive coefficients imply BGT0<0 => BHT0<0 and
   CFT0>0 => DFT0>0.

common hypothesis. All eight OP signs are fixed by the arc itself.

## 2. Complete finite sign catalogue

A row lists its successive actual-triangle sign choices. At each
nonterminal prefix the next named triangle is split into both strict
signs. The full list has no overlapping leaf and leaves no branch
uncovered; the seven binary-trie leaf counts are:

| Arc | Polynomial leaves | Geometric leaves | Total leaves |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| ABC | 13 | 0 | 13 |
| BCD | 14 | 0 | 14 |
| CDE | 17 | 0 | 17 |
| DEF | 9 | 1 | 10 |
| EFG | 14 | 0 | 14 |
| GHA | 13 | 1 | 14 |
| HAB | 13 | 0 | 13 |

There are two geometric leaves. For arc DEF, the path `FHO-` contradicts
O being inside the heptagon, whose FH edge test is positive. For arc
GHA, the path `EGP+,CGP-,BGO+,FHP-` contradicts the same positive FH
edge test at P. These are the only leaves without a polynomial sum.

The remaining rows are listed below. Their box flags N and T are
defined in Section 3. The suffix `gap` applies the additional coordinate
gap refinements there; a flag without that suffix uses the unrefined
box. This is merely a choice between proved closed boxes.

## 3. Rational boxes and affine factors

Put

    eta=93/790, ell=7347/69700, Fmax=1-ell.

For box N define

    h0=4s/Fmax, h1=1-s, f0=4s/(1-eta),
    x0=s/(h1-eta), e0=h0.

For box T define instead

    h0=257/500, h1=203/250, f0=Ks/(1-eta),
    x0=s/(h1-eta), e0=eta+s/(1-f0).

Box T is used only when the row's signs put one of O,P in AGH. In
coordinate order (a,b,c,d,e,f,g,h,z,x,y,u,v), the boxes are:

| Coordinate | N lower | N upper | T lower | T upper |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| a | eta | 1-4s | eta | 1-Ks |
| b | x0 | Fmax | x0 | Fmax |
| c | ell | Fmax | f0 | Fmax |
| d | eta | h1 | eta | h1 |
| e | e0 | 1 | e0 | 1 |
| f | f0 | Fmax | f0 | Fmax |
| g | x0 | 3/5 | 3x0 | 3/5 |
| h | h0 | h1 | h0 | h1 |
| z | h0+s | 1 | h0+s | 1 |
| x,y,u,v | x0,eta,x0,eta | Fmax,1-s,Fmax,1-s | x0,eta,x0,eta | Fmax,1-s,Fmax,1-s |

Box N is N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md's box, with g<3/5 from
N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md Section 2. For either heptagon-interior point,
AH clearance gives its abscissa at least x0, while

    (1-e)FHT0+(1-h)EFT0=EFH(1-y_T0),
    EFH<=(1-e)+(1-h)

gives y_T0<=1-s. The lower ordinate eta is N7_PLACEMENTS.md.

For box T, AGH>=3s and FGH>=s give

    Fmax(h-eta)(1-h)-3s(1-h)-s(h-eta)>=0.

The concave quadratic on the left has values
-216669513/1376575000000 and -87402983/344143750000 at
257/500 and 203/250 respectively; its derivative is positive at the
first endpoint and negative at the second. Thus h lies strictly
between these endpoints. The paired-ear inequality for AGH,FGH
also gives the endpoint-support area S'>=Gamma2*s>Ks. Since
S'<f(1-a), this proves the displayed a,f,c bounds. The AGH clearance
gives g>=3x0. CDE>=s and c>=f0 give e>=e0. These prove box T in full,
independently of which of O,P is its AGH occupant.

The gap refinements use only the following actual OP differences:

    d_H-d_A=(h-a)U,    d_E-d_D=(e-d)U,
    d_B-d_C=(c-b)V.

If A,H have opposite signs, set epsilon to the sign of d_H; then
`epsilon*U>=2x0`. If D,E have opposite signs, set epsilon to the sign
of d_E; then `epsilon*U>=2ell`. If B,C have opposite signs, set epsilon
to the sign of d_B; then `epsilon*V>=2eta`. The denominators are the
proved upper spans h1-eta, 697/790, and 79/100.

For rows marked `gap`, start with the indicated N or T box. Process
AH, DE, BC in that order, ignoring pairs whose signs agree. For a bound
U>=d replace upper_x by min(upper_x,upper_u-d) and then lower_u by
max(lower_u,lower_x+d); for -U>=d exchange x,u. For V>=d make the
same two replacements with y,v, and for -V>=d exchange y,v. These
ordered rational operations define the refined box completely. Each
endpoint is positive, and every resulting interval is nonempty.

For the row's final box define L_(2j)=coordinate_j-lower_j and
L_(2j+1)=upper_j-coordinate_j, j=0,...,12. The next affine factors are:

| Index | Factor | Index | Factor |
|---:|---|---:|---|
| 26 | h-a-s | 27 | 697/790-h+a |
| 28 | e-d-s | 29 | 697/790-e+d |
| 30 | c-b-117/1000 | 31 | 79/100-c+b |
| 32 | f-b | 33 | c-f |
| 34 | f-g | 35 | h-d |
| 36 | e-a | 37 | z-h-s |
| 38 | e-h | | |

Next append the valid OP-gap factors in order AH,DE,BC, with precisely
the signs and constants above. In box T append last
`g-x_T0-2x0`, for its actual AGH occupant T0. Indeed the two other
fan triangles at T0 have total area (g-x_T0)(h-a)>=2s. All these
factors are nonnegative, whether or not a row uses the gap-refined box.

## 4. Quadratic consequences of five convex points

We use the universal scale-free support lemma proved in
N7_THREE_FIVE_UPPER_HEIGHT.md Section 6. For five actual points in
strict convex cyclic order A0,B0,C0,D0,E0, put

    X=[A0 B0 E0], Y=[A0 D0 E0], Z=[A0 B0 D0].

Then

    XY-11s(X-Z)>=0.                                  (8)

For clarity, if X-Z<=0 this is immediate. Otherwise the supporting
rays A0B0 and E0D0 meet at

    T=A0+Y/(X-Z)(B0-A0)=E0+X/(X-Z)(D0-E0).

Both coefficients exceed one: X-(X-Z)=Z>0 and
Y-(X-Z)=[B0 D0 E0]>0. Thus their actual-endpoint triangle A0TE0
contains all five convex actual points. Its doubled area is XY/(X-Z),
and the convex-five endpoint theorem requires it to exceed 11s.
This proves (8) without any bound on the virtual supporting vertex.

We replace one factor in (8) by a proved upper bound. If X<=B, then

    B*Y-11s(X-Z)>=0;

if Y<=B, then

    11s*Z-(11s-B)*X>=0.                              (9)

All such factors are quadratic in the original point coordinates.
The following rules define the upper bound B for every hull triangle:
start at B=1, since it is a triangle in the unit square, and take the
minimum of every applicable entry below.

| Condition on the triangle | Upper bound |
|---|---|
| All its vertices belong to ABCD,CDEF,EFGH,or GHAB | 8s |
| All its vertices belong to HABC or FGHA | (1-s)^2 |
| All its vertices belong to BCDE or DEFG | 9s |
| It is one of AEF,BEF,ABE,ABF,CDG,CGH,CDH,DGH | 69/100 |
| It is one of ABC,ABH,BCD,CDE,AGH,FGH | 121/250 |
| It is DEF or EFG | 1-4s |

The first three lines are the global cap/container area bounds of
N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md, not merely its capacity conclusions. In particular
its actual-endpoint DEFG support triangle has area below 9s. The
fourth line is N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md. The last two lines are the
paired-ear and remaining-ear estimates in N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md; the
rational value 121/250 is justified in N7_LONG_TRIANGLE_CAPS.md.
An actual triangle contained in a cap has area at most that cap's area.

A factor named `F5_WORD_X` or `F5_WORD_Y` uses the five letters of WORD
in their printed cyclic order and the corresponding version of (9).
Every WORD below is a cyclic rotation of five distinct hull vertices;
thus its strict convexity is automatic. Here is the complete list of
these factors actually used, including the substituted rational bound.

## 5. Other nonnegative factors and actual containment witnesses

A signed actual triangle factor `XYZ+` means XYZ-s and `XYZ-` means
-XYZ-s, justified by the row's sign tables. The common support factors are

    EP=f(1-a)-4s, EL=hc-4s, ER=e(1-b)-4s,
    SUP=f^2(z-a)-fg(1-a)-4s(f-g),
    P_AHGFE=f(1-e)+(1-f)(1-a-11s),
    P_BCDEF=e(1-f)+(1-e)(1-b-11s),
    P_DEFGH=g(h-d)+z-h-11sg,
    P_HABCD=(h-11s)(1-c)+dc,
    P_ABCDE=(e-11s)b+a(1-b).

They are proved in N7_OP_LINE_CAPS.md. Its cap bounds give

    UW=8s-area(W), W=ABCD,CDEF,EFGH,GHAB,
    UBR=9s-e(1-b),
    UTR=9s(f-g)-(1-g)[(1-d)(f-g)+(1-z)(1-f)].

For any of the eight long triangles T, `UT` means 69/100-T.
The paired-ear estimates also supply

    EAR_ABH=121/250-ABH, EAR_AGH=121/250-AGH,
    PAIR_BL=hc-ABC-ABH-2s,
    PAIR_BR=e(1-b)-BCD-CDE-2s.

For an actual triangle T containing one or both of O,P, `CT_1` and
`CT_2` mean area(T)-3s and area(T)-Ks. For an actual cyclic
quadrilateral W containing both, `QW` means area(W)-8s. These are
the established triangle and quadrilateral occupancy bounds.

If both diagonals of W also separate O,P, `RW_IJ` means

    area(W)-(29/5)s-epsilon*(IJO-IJP),                (10)

where epsilon is the sign of IJO. The bracket is its positive actual
spread, and the threshold-free theorem QUADRILATERAL_SPREAD.md proves
(10), since 3+2sqrt(2)>29/5.

If one point occupies ABC or ABH, the paired-ear inequality implies
hc>=Gamma2*s>Ks; the factor `ELK` is hc-Ks. Similarly, an occupant
of BCD or CDE justifies `ERK=e(1-b)-Ks`. An AGH occupant justifies
`SUPK=f^2(z-a)-fg(1-a)-Ks(f-g)`. All relevant occupants are printed
in the following table, together with all triangle, quadrilateral,
and diagonal-spread witnesses used by the fixed sums. The edge tests
are the actual signs from Sections 1–2.

## 6. Fixed sums, exact bounds, and the complete conclusion

For each row form its printed positive integer sum W of products of
the nonnegative factors. Expand W=sum(c_alpha X^alpha). All coordinates
lie in the row's positive rational box, so

    W<=B(W)
      =sum_(c_alpha>0)c_alpha product_j(upper_j^alpha_j)
       +sum_(c_alpha<0)c_alpha product_j(lower_j^alpha_j).

The following exact rational values are negative and contradict W>=0.
The 93 polynomial leaves and two geometric leaves exhaust all seven
literal arcs at h<=e. Together with N7_THREE_FIVE_TOP.md and
N7_THREE_FIVE_UPPER_HEIGHT.md, this excludes every 3/5 line position
of the single-exterior N7 family.
